    ABOUT CGN MINING                                                         關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands and listed on          本公司於開曼群島註冊，在聯交所主板上
    the main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 01164). The            市（股票代號：01164）。本集團主要從事
    principal activities of the Group are natural uranium investment and     天然鈾資源的開發與貿易，旨在成為核電
    trading with a view to be one of the top suppliers of natural uranium    廠天然鈾資源的主要供應商之一。
    resources for nuclear power plants.


    The Company adheres to its core value of “Doing Things Right in One      本公司始終堅持「一次把事情做好」的核心
    Go” and takes “Safety First, Quality Foremost, Pursuing Excellence” as   價值觀，並以「安全第一，質量第一，追
    its working principle.                                                   求卓越」為工作原則。

    the PRC restarted after three years of “zero approval”. The approval     電在三年「零核准」後按下重啓鍵，多台新
    to construct several new nuclear power units means we may usher          核電機組的獲准開工建設意味著我們可
    in a recovery of nuclear power in the PRC. The overall trend is          能迎來了期待中的中國核電復甦。與此同
    improving globally as well, with nuclear power generation on the         時，世界核電發電量持續攀升，總體趨勢
    rise. This confirms our consistent view, that is “nuclear power as an    向好。這也印證了我們一直以來的看法，
    irreplaceable clean energy source, the development of which must go      「作為不可替代的清潔能源，核電的發展
    forward – and nuclear power in the PRC will have a promising future      一定是向上向前的，中國核電的發展大有
    development potential”. With positive signs of a growing nuclear         可為」。伴隨著核電行業積極信號釋放，
    power industry, gradual change may occur to the supply and demand        天然鈾行業供需格局或將緩慢改變，意味
    pattern for the natural uranium industry. The next few years will        著接下來幾年將是我們發展的重要機遇
    therefore present important opportunities for our development before     期，是天然鈾價格反轉前不可錯失的階
    the price of natural uranium reverses.                                   段。

    strategy.                                                                進積蓄人才。

improvements were made to corporate governance.

Nuclear fuel cycle market and industry analysis                           核燃料循環市場與行業研判

As far as the conversion market is concerned, the price increase in       就轉化市場而言，短期內價格漲幅大小很

and Metropolis conversion plant restarts its production, the              產，轉化價格應會趨於穩定。
conversion price will stabilise.


The uranium enrichment market faces larger uncertainties. The             濃縮市場存在較大的變數，美俄中止協議

three main risks in 2020:                                                  以下三項風險：


International natural uranium trade risks                                  國際天然鈾貿易風險

The uncertain price trend of natural uranium and the complexity of         考慮到天然鈾價格走勢的不確定性及國際

of contract by the counter party and failure to recover receivables.       收回等。


To reduce risks in natural uranium trading, the Company has taken          為降低天然鈾貿易風險，本公司從多方面
measures to strengthen trade risk management and control. Firstly,         加強貿易風險管控。一是組建專業團隊，
establish a professional team to closely track and analyse global          密切跟蹤並分析全球核電發展走勢、鈾礦

uranium mines and changes of the supply and demand in natural              加強天然鈾價格預測能力，把握市場低位
uranium, strengthen the ability on prediction of uranium price, and        採購機會；二是與國際信用評級機構合
grasp market opportunities to purchase natural uranium at a lower          作，對交易方資質和履約能力等進行全方
cost. Secondly, cooperate with international credit rating institutions    位監控，要求信用層級較低的交易方提供
to comprehensively monitor the qualifications and performance              保證函或預付款，降低貿易風險；三是持
capabilities of counterparties and require those counterparties            續加強內部管理，通過完善貿易敞口授權
with lower credit ratings to provide performance guarantees or             機制、合約方違約風險防控制度及信息系

     Financing model                                                          融資模式

     Given the complex and ever-changing financial market, the Company        在複雜多變的金融市場環境下，本公司一
     has been exploring diverse financing methods and strives to establish    直探索多元化的融資方式，努力建立短、
     a financing model with combination of short-, medium-and long-term       中、長期資金相互搭配、直接融資與間接
     capital, merged direct and indirect financing and multiple financing     融資相結合、多種渠道並舉的融資模式，
     channels to ensure the protection of a stable fund. In the process       為公司提供穩健的資金保障。在債務融資
     of debt financing, the Company has taken a balanced approach to          過程中，本公司始終遵循成本和安全兼
     both cost and safety. The Company is committed to the pursuit of a       顧的原則。公司致力追求具有競爭力的融
     competitive financing cost rather than the lowest one to ensure the      資成本，卻並不以最低的融資成本為唯一
     security of financing and the quality of service received.               目標，以免損害融資安全及接受的服務質
                                                                              量。

     For projects with large capital expenditures and sound expected          對於具備良好收益預算的大額資本性開支
     returns, the Company will prudently consider using equity financing to   項目，公司會謹慎地考慮採用股權融資平
     balance risks and enhance Shareholder value.                             衡風險，增進股東價值。

     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                財務風險管理
     The Group’s development comes with various financial risks, such as      本集團業務發展伴隨著各類財務風險，如
     debt risk and exchange rate risks.                                       債務風險、匯率風險等。

     Risk management of debt                                                  債務風險管理

     As at 31 December 2019, the borrowings from a fellow subsidiary of       於2019年12月31日，來自一間同系附屬
     the Company were HK$1,067 million. The loans were unsecured and          公司的借款為1,067百萬港元，該貸款為
     interest bearing ranging from 3.2% to 4.6% per annum.                    無抵押及年利率介乎3.2%至4.6%。

are reviewed by the Board from time to time.                             會不定期進行檢討。

industry experience, background, gender and other attributes of            會將考慮其成員的不同技能、區域和行業
the its members. All appointments are based on the talent, skill and       經驗、背景、性別以及其他特點。所有任
experience of the individual, as well as gender diversity, so as to meet   命均基於才幹、技能、經驗以及性別差異

performing reviews. Internal control department reports to the audit       部門每年度向審核委員會彙報一次有關內
committee once a year on key findings regarding internal controls.         部監控的主要調查結果，審核委員會向董

Voting by poll                                                             以點票方式進行投票

administrative matter to be voted by a show of hands.                      手方式進行表決。

                 class nuclear fuel supplier that provides safe, economical             流的核燃料供應商，為核電站

                 power plants. The Company strives to create a more                     料產品和服務，努力締造更繁

                 long-term benefits to the society while pursuing good                  在爭取業務表現和持續增長的
                 business performance and sustained growth.                             同時，亦能為社會帶來長遠裨
                                                                                        益。


                 In order to realize the Group’s corporate social                       為集團實踐其企業社會責任願

                 values of corporate social responsibility that reflect our             責任的核心價值，務求以「責
                 value orientation of “responsibility”, “rigorousness and               任擔當」、「嚴謹務實」、「創新
                 practicality”, “innovation”, “customer-oriented” and                   進取」、「客戶導向」、「價值
                 “creating value”. We manage all our production and                     創造」的價值取向，一切生產
                 operational activities based on the Group’s decision-                  和經營管理活動都以「安全第
                 making standards and the basic principles of “safety first”,           一」、「質量第一」、「追求卓
                 “quality foremost” and “pursuing excellence”, in order to              越」為集團決策基本標準和基
                 lead the Group towards its core value of “Doing Things                 本原則，引領集團邁向「一次
                 Right in One Go”.                                                      把事情做好」的核心價值。

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE                                          核數師對審核綜合財務報表之
AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                                         責任
STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether             我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from              存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an       陳述取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見

             AMENDMENTS TO HONG KONG                                                 務報告準則（「香港財務報

                                                                                         （續）
             (“HKFRS(s)”) (Continued)
             Impact on adoption of HKFRS 16 Leases                                   採納香港財務報告準則第16號租賃
                                                                                     之影響

             HKFRS 16 introduces new or amended requirements                         香港財務報告準則第16號引入有關
             with respect to lease accounting. It introduces significant             租賃會計的新訂或經修訂規定。其
             changes to the lessee accounting by removing the distinction            透過移除經營租賃與融資租賃之差
             between operating lease and finance lease and requiring the             異，並要求所有租賃確認使用權資
             recognition of right-of-use asset and a lease liability for all         產及租賃負債，從而對承租人會計
             leases, except for short-term leases and leases of low value            處理產生重大變動，惟短期租賃及
             assets. In contrast to lessee accounting, the requirements for          低值資產租賃除外。與承租人會計
             lessor accounting have remained largely unchanged. Details              處理相比，出租人會計處理之規定
             of these new accounting policies are described in note 3. The           大致維持不變。該等新會計政策之
             Group has applied HKFRS 16 Leases retrospectively with the              詳情載於附註3。本集團於2019年
             cumulative effect of initial application as an adjustment to the        1月1日已追溯應用香港財務報告準
             opening balance of equity, where appropriate, at 1 January              則第16號租賃作為首次應用的累計
             2019, and has not restated comparatives for the 2018                    影響，作為對權益期初結餘的調整
             reporting period as permitted under the specific transitional           （如適用），且並無重列該準則的特
             provisions in the standard. Accordingly, certain comparative            定過渡性條文所允許的2018年報
             information may not be comparable as comparative                        告期間的比較數字。因此，由於比
             information was prepared under HKAS 17 Leases.                          較資料乃根據香港會計準則第17號
                                                                                     租賃編製，若干比較資料可能無法
                                                                                     比較。


             On transition to HKFRS 16, the Group elected to apply the               過渡至香港財務報告準則第16號
             practical expedient to the assessment of which arrangements             時，本集團已選擇將實際權宜方法
             are, or contain, leases. It applied HKFRS 16 only to contracts          應用於已有或包含租賃安排的評估
             that were previously identified as leases. Contracts that were          中。其僅將香港財務報告準則第16
             not identified as leases under HKAS 17 and HK(IFRIC)-4 were             號應用於先前確認為租賃之合約。
             not reassessed. Therefore, the definition of a lease under              並無根據香港會計準則第17號及
             HKFRS 16 has been applied only to contracts entered into or             香港（國際財務報告詮釋委員會）第
             changed on or after 1 January 2019.                                     4號確定為租賃之合約並無重新評
                                                                                     估。因此，根據香港財務報告準則
                                                                                     第16號，租賃的定義僅適用於於
                                                                                     2019年1月1日或之後訂立或變更
                                                                                     之合約中。

     with remaining lease term of twelve months or less). These             產租賃及餘下期限為十二個月或以

                                                                            3.80%。


     The Group recognises right-of-use assets and measures                  本集團確認使用權資產並在以下情
     them at an amount equal to the lease liability, adjusted by the        況下計量：金額相等於租賃負債，
     amount of any prepaid or accrued lease payments.                       按任何預付或應計租賃款項金額作
                                                                            出調整。

                                                                            異。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     3.   主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in              綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
     accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition,                會頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。
     the consolidated financial statements include applicable                 此外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證
     disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of               券上市規則及香港《公司條例》適
     Securities on the Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong                    用之披露規定。
     Companies Ordinance.


     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on              綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編
     the historical cost basis, except for investment properties that         製，惟投資物業則按公允值計算。
     are measured at fair values.


     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本一般根據交換商品及服務
     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  提供代價之公允值計算。

      3.     SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                        3.   主要會計政策（續）
             (Continued)

             Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset             公允值為市場參與者於現時市況下
             or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between           於計量日在主要（或最有利）市場因
             market participants in the principal (or most advantageous)                 出售資產而收取或因轉讓負債而支
             market at the measurement date under current market                         付之價格（即平倉價），不論該價格
             conditions (i.e. an exit price) regardless of whether that price            是否直接觀察或利用其他估值方法
             is directly observable or estimated using another valuation                 估計。公允值計量詳情闡述於下文
             technique. Details of fair value measurement are explained in               載列之會計政策。主要會計政策載
             the accounting policies set out below.                                      於下文。


             The principal accounting policies are set out below.                        主要會計政策載於下文。


             Basis of consolidation                                                      綜合基準

             The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial             綜合財務報表包括本公司及受本公
             statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                    司所控制實體（即其附屬公司）之財
             Company (i.e. its subsidiaries). If a subsidiary prepares its               務報表。倘附屬公司編製其財務報
             financial statements using accounting policies other than                   表所採用之會計政策並非綜合財務
             those adopted in the consolidated financial statements for like             報表中就類似情況下之類似交易及
             transactions and events in similar circumstances, appropriate               事件採用之會計政策，則應在編製
             adjustments are made to that subsidiary’s financial statements              綜合財務報表時在該附屬公司之財
             in preparing the consolidated financial statements to ensure                務報表中作出適當調整以確保與本
             conformity with the Group’s accounting policies.                            集團所採取之會計政策相一致。


             Control is achieved where the Group has: (i) the power over                 倘本集團(i)對投資對象擁有權力；
             the investee; (ii) exposure, or rights, to variable returns from            (ii)對參與投資對象而獲得之可變回
             its involvement with the investee; and (iii) the ability to use its         報須承擔風險或擁有權利；及(iii)可
             power over the investee to affect the amount of the Group’s                 對投資對象行使其權力以影響本集
             returns. When the Group has less than a majority of the                     團之回報金額，則獲得控制權。倘
             voting rights of an investee, power over the investee may be                本集團於投資對象之投票權未能佔
             obtained through: (i) a contractual arrangement with other vote             大多數，則可根據所有相關因素及
             holders; (ii) rights arising from other contractual arrangements;           情況通過以下途徑取得對投資對象
             (iii) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights; or (iv)        之權力：(i)與其他投票權持有人訂
             a combination of the above, based on all relevant facts and                 立合約安排；(ii)其他合約安排產生
             circumstances.                                                              之權利；(iii)本集團之投票權及潛在
                                                                                         投票權；或(iv)上述途徑之組合。


             The Company reassesses whether it controls an investee if                   倘相關事實及情況表明上述控制權
             facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to                  之相關元素中一項或多項元素發生
             one or more of these elements of control stated above.                      變動，則本公司會重估是否其對投
                                                                                         資對象具有控制權。

     Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                          （續）
                                                                              綜合基準

     Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Company                    當本公司獲得附屬公司之控制權
     obtains control of the subsidiary and ceases when the Group              時，附屬公司之合併開始而當本集
     loses control of the subsidiary.                                         團失去附屬公司之控制權時則附屬
                                                                              公司之合併終止。


     Income and expenses of subsidiaries are included in                      於本集團取得控制權日期直至本集
     the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                   團不再控制附屬公司日期期間，附
     comprehensive income from the date the Group gains                       屬公司之收支計入綜合損益及其他
     control until the date when the Group ceases to control the              全面收益表。
     subsidiary.

     Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                 附屬公司之損益及其他全面收益之
     income of subsidiaries are attributed to the owners of                   各部分歸屬於本公司擁有人及非
     the Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total                  控股權益。附屬公司之全面收益總
     comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to the                額歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
     owners of the Company and to the non-controlling interests               益，即使此情況導致非控股權益產
     even if this results in the non-controlling interests having a           生虧絀結餘。
     deficit balance.

     All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses         與本集團實體間之往來交易有關的
     and cash flows relating to transactions between entities of the          所有集團內資產及負債、權益、收
     Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                           入、開支及現金流量於合併時全數
                                                                              抵銷。


     Merger accounting for business combination involving                     共同控制實體業務合併的合併會計
     entities under common control                                            法

     The consolidated financial statements include the financial              綜合財務報表包括發生共同控制合
     statements items of the combining entities or businesses                 併的合併實體或業務的財務報表
     in which the common control combination occurs as if the                 項目，猶如自該等合併實體或業務
     combination had occurred from the date when the combining                首次受控制方控制日期起已進行合
     entities or businesses first came under the control of the               併。
     controlling party.

     The net assets of the combining entities or businesses                   從控制方的角度看，合併實體或業
     are consolidated using the existing book values from the                 務的淨資產按照現有賬面價值進行
     controlling party’s perspective. No amount is recognised as              合併。在控制方的權益持續的條件
     consideration for goodwill or excess of acquirer’s interest in           下，當發生共同控制合併時，不確
     the net fair value of acquiree’s identifiable assets, liabilities        認商譽或者收購方所佔被收購方可
     and contingent liabilities over cost at the time of common               辨認資產、負債及或有負債的公允
     control combination, to the extent of the continuation of the            淨值的權益超出成本部分的金額。
     controlling party’s interest.

             The consolidated statement of profit or loss, consolidated              綜合損益表、綜合損益及其他全面
             statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income              收益表包括自最早呈報日期起或者
             includes the results of each of the combining entities or               自合併實體或業務首次受共同控制
             businesses from the earliest date presented or since the date           日起（兩者之間較短者）的每一個合
             when the combining entities or businesses first came under              併實體或業務的業績，而不論共同
             the common control, where this is a shorter period, regardless          控制合併日期。
             of the date of the common control combination.


             The comparative amounts in the consolidated financial                   綜合財務報表中呈列的比較金額，
             statements are presented as if the entities or businesses had           是猶如該等實體或業務於上一報
             been combined at the end of the previous reporting period               告期末已合併，惟合併實體或業務
             unless the combining entities or businesses first came under            首次受共同控制時（以較後日期 為
             common control at a later date.                                         準）則除外。


             Investments in an associate and a joint venture                         於一間聯營公司及一間合營企業之
                                                                                     投資

             An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant          聯營公司指本集團能對其行使重大
             influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in         影響力。重大影響力指可參與受投
             the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but        資公司之財務及營運決策但不是控
             is not control or joint control of those policies.                      制或共同控制該等政策之權力。


             A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties              合營企業指一項共同安排，對安排
             that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to               擁有共同控制權之訂約方據此對安
             the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the           排之資產淨值擁有權利。共同控制
             contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,              是指按照合約約定對某項安排所共
             which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities          有的控制，共同控制僅在當相關活
             require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.               動要求共同享有控制權之各方作出
                                                                                     一致同意之決定時存在。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                       3.   主要會計政策（續）
     (Continued)

     Investments in an associate and a joint venture                            於一間聯營公司及一間合營企業之
     (Continued)                                                                 （續）
                                                                                投資

     The Group’s investments in an associate and a joint venture                本集團於一間聯營公司及一間合營
     are accounted for in the consolidated financial statements                 企業的投資於綜合財務報表入賬內
     using the equity method. Under the equity method,                          採用權益法。根據權益法，於聯營
     investments in an associate and a joint venture are initially              公司及合營企業的投資初始按成本
     recognised at cost. The Group’s share of the profit or loss                確認。本集團應佔聯營公司及合營
     and changes in the other comprehensive income of the                       企業的損益及其他全面收益之變動
     associate and joint venture are recognised in profit or loss and           分別於收購日期後於損益及其他全
     other comprehensive income respectively after the date of                  面收益內確認。倘本集團應佔一間
     acquisition. If the Group’s share of losses of an associate or             聯營公司或合營企業的虧損等於或
     a joint venture equals or exceeds its interest in the associate            超過本集團於該聯營公司或合營企
     or joint venture, which determined using the equity method                 業的權益（使用權益法釐定連同實
     together with any long-term interests that, in substance,                  質屬於本集團於聯營及合營企業的
     form part of the Group’s net investment in the associate or                投資淨值的任何長期權益）時，本
     joint venture, the Group discontinues recognising its share of             集團不再確認其應佔的進一步虧
     further losses. Additional losses are provided for, and a liability        損。本集團僅在已招致法定或推定
     is recognised, only to the extent that the Group has incurred              責任或代表聯營及合營企業支付款
     legal or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf               項的情況下確認額外虧損或負債。
     of the associate or joint venture.


     If an associate or a joint venture uses accounting policies                就類似交易及於類似情況下之事
     other than those of the Group for like transactions and events             件，倘聯營公司或合營企業使用的
     in similar circumstances, adjustments are made to make the                 會計政策有別於本集團所採納者，
     associate’s or joint venture’s accounting policies conform to              則在權益法下本集團使用聯營公司
     those of the Group when the associate’s or joint venture’s                 或合營企業之財務報表時，須調整
     financial statements are used by the Group in applying the                 聯營及合營企業之會計政策，以符
     equity method.                                                             合本集團之會計政策。


     An investment in an associate or a joint venture is accounted              於被投資方成為一間聯營公司或合
     for using the equity method from the date on which the                     營企業當日，於聯營公司或合營企
     investee becomes an associate or a joint venture. On                       業的投資採用權益法入賬。於收購
     acquisition of the investment, any excess of the cost of                   投資後，投資成本超過本集團應佔
     acquisition over the Group’s share of the net fair value of                聯營公司或合營企業可識別資產及
     the identifiable assets and liabilities of the associate or joint          負債之公允淨值部分確認為商譽，
     venture is recognised as goodwill and is included in the                   並計入投資之賬面值。
     carrying amount of the investment.

     prospective basis.

     Impairment losses on tangible assets                                    有形資產之減值虧損

     At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the               本集團於報告期結束時評估其有形
     carrying amounts of its tangible assets with finite useful lives        資產有限可使用之賬面值，以確定
     to determine whether there is any indication that those assets          該等資產有否出現減值虧損跡象。
     have suffered an impairment loss. If any such indication exists,        倘有任何該等跡象，則會估計資
     the recoverable amount of the asset is estimated in order to            產的可收回金額，以釐定減值虧損
     determine the extent of the impairment loss, if any.                    （如有）的程度。


     When it is not possible to estimate the recoverable amount              倘無法估計個別資產之可收回金
     of an individual asset, the Group estimates the recoverable             額，本集團估計資產所屬現金產生
     amount of the cash-generating unit to which the asset                   單位之可收回金額。如可識別合理
     belongs. When a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation           及一致之分配基準，則公司資產亦
     can be identified, corporate assets are also allocated to               被分配至個別現金產生單位，或於
     individual cash-generating units, or otherwise they are                 其他情況下彼等被分配至已識別合
     allocated to the smallest group of cash-generating units for            理及一致分配基準之現金產生單位
     which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis can be               之最小組合。
     identified.


     Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs               可收回金額為公允值減去處置成本
     of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the            及使用價值兩者中之較高者。於評
     estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present             估使用價值時，估計未來現金流量
     value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market        乃以稅前貼現率貼現至現值，該貼
     assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific           現率能反映當前市場所評估之貨幣
     to the asset for which the estimates of future cash flows have          時間值及資產特定風險（未來現金
     not been adjusted.                                                      流量估計尚未就此作出調整）。


     If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-generating             倘估計一項資產（或現金產生單位）
     unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the             之可收回金額低於其賬面值，則該
     carrying amount of the asset (or cash-generating unit) is               項資產（或現金產生單位）之賬面值
     reduced to its recoverable amount. An impairment loss is                即減至其可收回金額。減值虧損即
     recognised immediately in profit or loss.                               時於損益中確認。


     Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying            於其後撥回減值虧損時，資產（或
     amount of the asset (or cash-generating unit) is increased to           現金產生單位）之賬面值將調高至
     the revised estimate of its recoverable amount, but so that             其經修訂估計之可收回金額，惟經
     the increased carrying amount does not exceed the carrying              增加之賬面值不得高於在過往年
     amount that would have been determined had no impairment                度並無確認減值虧損下就該資產
     loss been recognised for the asset (or cash-generating unit) in         （或現金產生單位）原應釐定之賬面
     prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is recognised as          值。撥回之減值虧損即時於收入中
     income immediately.                                                     確認。

             Revenue recognition                                                      收入確認

             Revenue is recognised to depict the transfer of promised                 確認收入乃描述已承諾服務對客戶
             services to customers in an amount that reflects the                     的轉移，而確認金額乃反映實體預
             consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled in              期從該等服務應得的代價。具體而
             exchange for those services. Specifically, the Group uses a              言，本集團使用五個步驟來確認收
             5-step approach to revenue recognition:                                  入：


             •       Step 1: Identify the contract(s) with customer                   •    第一步：識別與客戶訂立
                                                                                           的合約


             •       Step 2: Identify the performance obligations in the              •    第二步：識別合約中的履
                     contract                                                              約責任


             •       Step 3: Determine the transaction price                          •    第三步： 釐定交易價


             •       Step 4: Allocate the transaction price to the                    •    第四步：將交易價分配至
                     performance obligations in the contract                               合約中的履約責任


             •       Step 5: Recognise revenue when (or as) the entity                •    第五步：於實體完成履約
                     satisfies a performance obligation                                    責任時確認收入


             The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                  本集團於完成履約責任時確認收
             obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods                入，即於特定履約責任相關的貨品
             underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred          的「控制權」轉讓予客戶時。
             to customers.


             A performance obligation represents a good or service (or                履約責任指個別的貨品或服務（或
             a bundle of goods or services) that is district or a series of           一組貨品或服務）或一系列大致相
             distinct goods or services that are substantially same.                  同的個別貨品或服務。


             Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised               倘符合以下其中一項標準，則控制
             over time by reference to the progress towards complete                  權隨時間轉移，而收入則參照完全
             satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of the        履行相關履約責任之進展情況而隨
             following criteria is met:                                               時間確認：

     Taxation                                                                稅項

     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently              所得稅支出指應付即期稅項及遞延
     payable and deferred tax.                                               稅項之總和。


     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the            應付即期稅項乃按本年度應課稅溢
     year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported         利計算。應課稅溢利與綜合損益表
     in the consolidated statement of profit or loss because it              中所報除稅前溢利不同，原因為前
     excludes items of income or expense that are taxable or                 者不包括在其他年度應課稅或可扣
     deductible in other years and it further excludes items that are        稅收入或開支，且不包括毋須課稅
     never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current          或不可扣稅之項目。本集團之即期
     tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or             稅項負債乃按報告期間結束前已頒
     substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.               佈或實際已頒佈之稅率計算。


     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                     遞延稅項乃就於綜合財務報表所示
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the           資產及負債之賬面值與計算應課
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax             稅溢利所用相應稅基間臨時差額確
     bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax           認。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課
     liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary          稅臨時差額確認，而遞延稅項資產
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised               則於可能可動用應課稅溢利以抵銷
     for all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is        可扣減臨時差額之情況下確認。若
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which           於一項交易中，因商譽或因業務合
     those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such            併以外原因初步確認其他資產及負
     deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the           債而引致不會影響應課稅溢利亦不
     temporary difference arises from goodwill or from the initial           會影響會計溢利之臨時差額，則不
     recognition (other than in a business combination) of other             會確認遞延稅項資產及負債。
     assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the
     taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

             Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary            遞延稅項負債乃按於附屬公司、聯
             differences associated with investments in subsidiaries, an              營公司及合營企業投資之應課稅臨
             associate and a joint venture, except where the Group is able            時差額確認，惟倘本集團可控制臨
             to control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is            時差額撥回及臨時差額未必於可見
             probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the           將來撥回之情況則除外。有關該等
             foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible          投資及權益之可扣減臨時差額所產
             temporary differences associated with such investments and               生遞延稅項資產，僅於具有足夠應
             interests are only recognised to the extent that it is probable          課稅溢利可動用暫時差異之利益，
             that there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to           且預期於可見將來撥回時確認。
             utilise the benefits of the temporary differences and they are
             expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.


             The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the            遞延稅項資產的賬面值於每一報告
             end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that              期末予以審閱，如不可能再有足夠
             it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be         應課稅溢利可供收回全部或部分資
             available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.             產，則將削減遞延稅項的賬面值。


             Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates        遞延稅項資產及負債乃根據於報告
             that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability          期末已頒佈或實際已頒佈之稅率
             is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates (and tax         （及稅法），按預期於清償負債或變
             laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the             現資產期間適用之稅率計算。
             end of the reporting period.


             The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects          遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映本
             the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                集團預期於報告期末收回或償還資
             which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period,             產及負債賬面值產生之稅務後果。
             to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and
             liabilities.


             For the purposes of measuring deferred tax liabilities or                就計量採用公允值模式計量的投資
             deferred tax assets for investment properties that are                   物業的遞延稅項負債或遞延稅項資
             measured using the fair value model, the carrying amounts of             產而言，該等物業的賬面值乃假設
             such properties are presumed to be recovered entirely through            可透過銷售全部收回，除非該假設
             sale, unless the presumption is rebutted. The presumption is             不再成立。當投資物業可予折舊及
             rebutted when the investment property is depreciable and is              於本集團的業務模式（其業務目標
             held within a business model whose objective is to consume               是隨時間而非透過銷售消耗投資物
             substantially all of the economic benefits embodied in the               業所包含的絕大部分經濟利益）內
             investment property over time, rather than through sale. If the          持有時，有關假設會不成立。倘有
             presumption is rebutted, deferred tax liabilities and deferred           關假設不成立，則上述投資物業的
             tax assets for such investment properties are measured in                遞延稅項負債及遞延稅項資產根據
             accordance with the general principles above.                            上述一般原則計量。

             entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional               時，以該實體功能貨幣以外之貨
             currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are recorded in               幣（外幣）進行之交易均按交易日期

             primary economic environment in which the entity operates)                 （即該實體經營所在主要經濟地區

             retranslated at the rates prevailing at the date when the                  史成本計量之非貨幣項目毋須重新
             fair value was determined. Non-monetary items that are                     換算。
             measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are

             recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.            損益確認。來自重新換算按公允值

             retranslation of non-monetary items in respect of which gains              新換算除外，在該情況下，匯兌差

             income, in which cases, the exchange differences are also

     Leasing                                                                   租賃

     Policy applicable on or after 1 January 2019                              於2019年 1月 1日或之後適用的政
                                                                               策

     Definition of a lease                                                     租賃的定義

     Under HKFRS 16, a contract is, or contains, a lease if the                根據香港財務報告準則第16號，倘
     contract conveys a right to control the use of an identified asset        合約賦予權利於一段時間內控制使
     for a period of time in exchange for consideration.                       用已識別資產以換取代價，則合約
                                                                               為或包含租賃。


     The Group as lessee                                                       本集團作為承租人

     The Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease,             本集團於合約開始時評估其是否或
     at inception of the contract. The Group recognises a right-of-            包含租賃。本集團就其作為承租人
     use asset and a corresponding lease liability with respect to all         的所有租賃安排確認使用權資產及
     lease arrangements in which it is the lessee, except for short-           相應租賃負債，惟短期租賃（界定
     term leases (defined as leases with a lease term of 12 months             為租賃年期為 12 個月或以下的租
     or less) and leases of low value assets. For these leases,                賃）及低價值資產租賃除外。就該
     the Group recognises the lease payments as an operating                   等租賃而言，本集團於租賃期內以
     expense on a straight-line basis over the term of the lease               直線法確認租賃付款為經營開支，
     unless another systematic basis is more representative of the             除非另一種系統化基準更能代表使
     time pattern in which economic benefits from the leased assets            用租賃資產所得之經濟利益的時間
     are consumed.                                                             模式。


     Lease liabilities                                                         租賃負債

     At the commencement date, the Group measures lease                        於開始日期，本集團按當日尚未支
     liability at the present value of the lease payments that are not         付的租賃付款之現值計量租賃負
     paid at that date. The lease payments are discounted by using             債。租賃付款採用租賃內隱含的利
     the interest rate implicit in the lease. If this rate cannot be           率貼現。倘無法輕易釐定該利率，
     readily determined, the Group uses its incremental borrowing              本集團則使用其增量借貸利率。
     rate.

             element and building element of the lease at the inception of

     When measuring fair value except for the Group’s share-                  當就減值評估目的而評估除本集團
     based payment transactions, leasing transactions, net                    股本結算之股份付款交易外之公

     if market participants would take those characteristics into             負債定價時所考慮之資產或負債特

     date.

     in its highest and best use.                                             而能夠產生經濟利益之能力。

                            indirectly observable.                                                      輸入數據之最低層級）。

                                                                                                        據之最低層級）。

             Amortised cost and effective interest method                                 攤銷成本及實際利率法

             The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                 實際利率法乃計算債務工具攤銷成
             amortised cost of a debt instrument and of allocating interest               本及按有關期間攤分利息收入之方
             income over the relevant period.                                             法。


             For financial assets other than purchased or originated credit-              對於除購入或承擔信貸已減值的
             impaired financial assets (i.e. assets that are credit-impaired              金融資產（即於初次確認時承擔信
             on initial recognition), the effective interest rate is the rate that        貸已減值的金融資產）外的金融資
             exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts (including                  產，實際利率法乃按債務工具預
             all fees and points paid or received that form an integral part              計全期或（如適用）較短期間，準備
             of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and other                  貼現估計未來現金收入（包括構成
             premiums or discounts) excluding expected credit losses,                     實際利率不可或缺部分之一切已付
             through the expected life of the debt instrument, or, where                  或已收費用及點數、交易成本及其
             appropriate, a shorter period, to the gross carrying amount                  他溢價或折讓，不包括預期信貸虧
             of the debt instrument on initial recognition. For purchased or              損）至其初步確認時債務工具之賬
             originated credit-impaired financial assets, a credit-adjusted               面總值之利率。對於購入或承擔信
             effective interest rate is calculated by discounting the estimated           貸已減值的金融資產，信貸調整實
             future cash flows, including expected credit losses, to the                  際利率法乃按估計未來現金流量
             amortised cost of the debt instrument on initial recognition.                （包括預期信貸虧損）貼現至初始確
                                                                                          認時債務工具攤銷成本的方式計
                                                                                          算。


             The amortised cost of a financial asset is the amount at which               金融資產的攤銷成本指金融資產於
             the financial asset is measured at initial recognition minus the             初始確認時計量的金額減去本金
             principal repayments, plus the cumulative amortisation using                 還款，加上初始金額與到期金額間
             the effective interest method of any difference between that                 的任何差額使用實際利率法計算的
             initial amount and the maturity amount, adjusted for any loss                累計攤銷（就任何虧損撥備作出調
             allowance. The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is                 整）。金融資產的賬面總值指金融
             the amortised cost of a financial asset before adjusting for any             資產就任何虧損撥備作出調整前的
             loss allowance.                                                              攤銷成本。

     assets which are subject to impairment under HKFRS                          對應之壞賬撥備按照香港財務報告
     9 (including rental deposits trade and other receivables,                   準則第9號項下之減值（包括租賃按

     date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition of         存）。預期信貸虧損之金額於每報
     financial instrument.                                                       告日期更新以反映自金融資產初始

     with significant balances, estimated based on the Group’s                   信貸虧損經驗就有重大結餘的客戶
     historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors that are            作個別估算，但須根據適用於債務

     an assessment of both the current as well as the forecast                   報告日對當前及預測方向（包括金

     For all other financial instruments, the Group measures the                 就所有其他金融工具而言，本集團

     Significant increase in credit risk                                         信貸風險顯著上升

     In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly            評估信貸風險是否自初始確認顯著
     since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a                 上升時，本集團比較金融工具於報
     default occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting           告日期發生違約的風險與金融工具
     date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial                  於初始確認日期發生違約的風險。
     instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In making                 作出評估時，本集團會考慮合理及
     this assessment, the Group considers both quantitative and                  可靠的定量及定性資料，包括過往
     qualitative information that is reasonable and supportable,                 經驗及無需付出過多成本或努力即
     including historical experience and forward-looking information             可得的前瞻性資料。
     that is available without undue cost or effort.

             Measurement and recognition of ECL                                        預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

             the probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude        概率、違約損失率（即違約時的損
             of the loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.           失程度）及違約風險的函數。評估
             The assessment of the probability of default and loss given               違約概率及違約損失率乃依據過往
             default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-looking           數據，並按前瞻性資料調整。而金
             information. As for the exposure at default, for financial assets,        融資產的違約風險暴露則由資產於
             this is represented by the assets’ gross carrying amount at the           報告日期的賬面總值代表。
             reporting date.

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the              實際利率法乃計算金融負債攤銷成
     amortised cost of a financial liability and of allocating interest        本及按相關期間攤分利息開支之
     expense over the relevant period. The effective interest                  方法。實際利率為在金融負債之預
     rate is the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash             計年期或（視適用情況而定）較短期
     payments (including all fees and points paid or received that             間，使估計未來現金支出（包括所
     form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction         有構成實際利率組成部分之已付或
     costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected               已收費用及利率點、交易成本及其
     life of the financial liability, or (where appropriate) a shorter         他溢價或折讓）準確貼現至一項金
     period, to the amortised cost of a financial liability.                   融負債的攤銷成本之利率。

     The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only               本集團於及僅於其責任獲解除後、
     when, the Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or                取消或到期時方終止確認金融負
     they expire. The difference between the carrying amount of                債。終止確認之金融負債賬面值與
     the financial liability derecognised and the consideration paid           已付或應付代價（包括任何已轉讓
     and payable, including any non-cash assets transferred or                 的非現金資產或已承擔的非現金負
     liabilities assumed, is recognised in profit or loss.                     債）之差額確認為損益。

     reported and disclosures made in the consolidated financial               設。估計及相關假設乃根據過往經

      5.     CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           5.   資本風險管理
             The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the           本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團
             Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                旗下實體能夠持續經營業務，並透
             maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation         過更完善平衡債務及權益，以為股
             of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy           東帶來最大回報。本集團整體策略
             remains unchanged from prior year.                                     與去年維持不變。


             The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts, which        本集團之資本結構包括淨負債（包
             includes loans from a fellow subsidiary and leases liabilities,        括應收一間同系附屬公司貸款及租
             net of cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to            賃負債），扣除現金及現金等值項
             owners of the Company, comprising issued share capital                 目以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（當
             and reserves. The directors of the Company review the                  中包括已發行股本及儲備）。本公
             capital structure on a regular basis. As a part of this review,        司董事定期檢討資本結構。作為檢
             the directors of the Company consider the cost of capital              討其中一環，本公司董事考慮資本
             and the risks associated with each class of capital, and take          成本及與各類資本相關的風險，並
             appropriate actions to adjust the Group’s capital structure.           就此採取適當行動調整本集團資本
                                                                                    結構。

   The subsidiaries in Hong Kong operate defined contribution        香港附屬公司為合資格僱員按香港
   schemes (the “MPF Scheme”) under the Hong Kong                    強制性公積金計劃條例推行定額供
   Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance which                  款計劃（
                                                                        「強積金計劃」
                                                                              ）。該等計劃
   are available to qualified employees. The assets of the           之資產與附屬公司之資產於獨立管
   schemes are held separately from those of the subsidiaries        理基金內分開持有。附屬公司之每
   in independently administered funds. Monthly contributions        月供款乃按照有關規定所訂明以適
   made by the subsidiaries are calculated based on certain          用薪酬成本之若干百分比或固定款
   percentages of the applicable payroll costs or fixed sums as      額（視適用情況而定）計算。僱員須
   stipulated under the relevant requirements, as appropriate.       按其月薪之5%或最高1,500港元作
   The employees are required to contribute 5% of their monthly      出供款，而彼等可選擇作出額外供
   salaries or up to a maximum of HK$1,500 and they can              款。僱員於65歲退休、身故或完全
   choose to make additional contributions. The employees are        喪失工作能力時享有100%之僱主
   entitled to 100% of the employer’s Mandatory Contributions        強制性供款。
   upon their retirement at the age of 65, death or total
   incapacity.


   Pursuant to the regulations of the relevant authorities in        根據中國及英國有關當局之規例，
   the PRC and UK, the subsidiaries of the Company in those          本公司之中國附屬公司參與各項政
   countries participate in respective government retirement         府退休福利計劃（
                                                                            「該等計劃」
                                                                                 ），據
   benefit schemes (the “Schemes”) whereby the subsidiaries are      此，該等附屬公司須向該等計劃作
   required to contribute to the Schemes to fund the retirement      出供款，以撥付合資格僱員之退休
   benefits of the eligible employees. Contributions made to the     福利。向該等計劃作出之供款乃按
   Schemes are calculated based on certain percentages of the        照中國及英國規例所訂明適用薪酬
   applicable payroll costs as stipulated under the requirements     成本之特定百分比計算。中國及英
   in the PRC and UK. The relevant authorities of the PRC and        國有關當局負責向退休僱員支付應
   UK are responsible for the entire pension obligations payable     付之全數退休福利。本集團就該等
   to the retired employees. The only obligation of the Group        計劃之責任僅為根據該等計劃持續
   with respect to the Schemes is to pay the ongoing required        作出所需供款。
   contributions under the Schemes.


   The retirement benefit schemes contribution represent gross       退休福利計劃供款即本集團向由中
   contributions by the Group to the Schemes operated by             國及英國有關當局運作之該等計劃
   the relevant authorities of the PRC and UK and the defined        以及於香港運作之定額供款計劃作
   contribution schemes operated in Hong Kong.                       出之供款總額。


   The total cost charged to profit or loss of HK$744,000 (2018:     自損益扣除之總成本744,000港元

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (Continued)                                    19. 投資物業（續）
    The carrying value of investment properties shown above are             上述賬面值之投資物業乃位於中
    situated in the PRC and held under medium-term lease.                   國，根據中期租賃持有。


    All of the Group’s property interests to earn rentals or for            本集團所有持有以賺取租金或作資
    capital appreciation purposes are measured using the                    本增值用途之物業權益均以公允值
    fair value model and are classified and accounted for as                模式計量，並分類及入賬列作投資
